touhou_sync_aircraft_designer = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				original_tag = ALI
				original_tag = DES
				original_tag = HAK
				original_tag = DLD
				original_tag = HEL
				original_tag = SSS
				original_tag = OPP
				original_tag = RAB
				original_tag = TEM
				original_tag = BLQ
				original_tag = KAP
				original_tag = TEN
				original_tag = HUM
				original_tag = VAM
				original_tag = MLS
				original_tag = EVI
			}
			has_dlc = "By Blood Alone"
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_dlc = "By Blood Alone"
				OR = {
					original_tag = ALI
					original_tag = DES
					original_tag = HAK
					original_tag = DLD
					original_tag = HEL
					original_tag = SSS
					original_tag = OPP
					original_tag = RAB
					original_tag = TEM
					original_tag = BLQ
					original_tag = KAP
					original_tag = TEN
					original_tag = HUM
					original_tag = VAM
					original_tag = MLS
					original_tag = EVI
				}
				has_country_flag = touhou_country_flag_demonforce_first_research
				OR = {
					AND = {
						has_dlc = "By Blood Alone"
						has_tech = iw_small_airframe
					}
					AND = {
						NOT = {
							has_dlc = "By Blood Alone"
						}
						has_tech = early_fighter
					}
				}
				NOT = {
					has_tech = touhou_air_license_demonforce_small_0
				}
			}
			set_technology = {
				touhou_air_license_demonforce_small_0 = 1
				popup = no
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_tech = touhou_air_license_demonforce_small_0

touhou_sync_vanilla_military = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = touhou_migration_initialized
			}
		}
		add_dynamic_modifier = {
			modifier = touhou_migration_touhou_demonforce_improved_special_forces
		}
		add_dynamic_modifier = {
			modifier = touhou_migration_touhou_demonforce_extreme_environment_training
